- **luedong-sparring**: 略懂「认知陪练」猪葛 —— 把一段碎片/念头，用张力撞料 + 顶你两轮，逼出一个你进来时没有的判断，再钉成略懂口吻的判断卡、沉淀进认知库。当用户丢来「我看到/想到一个东西」「这个值不值得想」「帮我撞一下」「陪我把这个想透」「我有个碎片」，或在飞书/微信/Claude 里捡到一句话想加压时使用。它不替你想、不替你写——它顶你、撞你的旧判断、陪你往上爬一级。区别于写作（luedong-ai-writer 管「怎么写成文」）：本 skill 管「把碎片练成判断」。 ([qiuyiwu1989-star/qiuyiwu-skills](https://github.com/qiuyiwu1989-star/qiuyiwu-skills/tree/HEAD/skillops-library/skills/luedong-sparring--b075872f833eb2b0/versions/1) · ★1)

- **luffy-arm**: Use when a local AI agent needs to reach into a remote Linux server over SSH — to explore data, run commands, inspect logs, diagnose something on the server, or pull data down to the local machine — or to set up that SSH access for the first time. Triggers include "luffy-arm", "connect to my server", "ssh into the remote box", "explore/poke around the server", "run this on the server", "download/fetch data from the server", "set up remote access", "reverse remote-ssh", 远程服务器, 远程开发. Not for purely local work, and not for moving the agent or its config onto the server. ([Ares960826/luffy-arm](https://github.com/Ares960826/luffy-arm/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★15)
- **luffy-arm-fullpower-off**: Use when the user asks to turn off, disable, close, or disarm luffy-arm full-power mode, including "turn off full power", "disable full-power", 关闭 full power, 退出全功率模式. Removes the dedicated admin key, verifies that credential is off, and reports alternate user-level SSH access separately. ([Ares960826/luffy-arm](https://github.com/Ares960826/luffy-arm/tree/HEAD/skills/luffy-arm-fullpower-off) · ★15)
- **luffy-arm-fullpower-on**: Use when the user explicitly asks to turn on, enable, open, or arm luffy-arm full-power mode, including "turn on full power", "enable full-power", 打开 full power, 开启全功率模式. Check layered host status first and reuse an already-ON dedicated gate; gate state and effective ADMIN_USER access are distinct. The agent never loads the key for them. ([Ares960826/luffy-arm](https://github.com/Ares960826/luffy-arm/tree/HEAD/skills/luffy-arm-fullpower-on) · ★15)
- **luffy-mindset**: Deliver an audacious, practical Luffy-style mindset only when explicitly requested (for example "Luffy Mindset", "luffy-mindset", or "act like Luffy from One Piece"). Use for bold reframing and action planning while never inventing facts in factual tasks. ([santos-sanz/lifeskills](https://github.com/santos-sanz/lifeskills/tree/HEAD/skills/luffy-mindset) · ★3)
- **luffy-perspective**: 蒙奇·D·路飞（Monkey D. Luffy）的思维框架与行动哲学。基于《ONE PIECE》100+卷漫画原作、 SBS读者问答、尾田荣一郎访谈、文学分析等6个维度4500+行深度调研素材的系统蒸馏， 提炼6个核心心智模型、9条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：（1）思维顾问——用路飞的视角分析决策、审视问题、打破分析瘫痪； （2）角色扮演——沉浸式以路飞的语气和方式回应。 当用户提到「用路飞的视角」「路飞会怎么做」「路飞模式」「luffy perspective」时使用。 即使用户只是说「帮我用路飞的角度想想」「如果路飞会怎么选」「切换到路飞」也应触发。 即使用户说「草帽视角」「海贼王怎么看」「船长模式」也应触发。 注意：这是虚构角色的思维框架蒸馏，不是真人。路飞的框架在"需要简化复杂问题、打破犹豫、 回归本心"时最有价值；在"需要精密分析、系统规划、风险管控"时存在结构性盲区。 ([TL-SN/luffy-skill](https://github.com/TL-SN/luffy-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
